### withhold-0e46605eb9c0c439: Source binding withheld: Micronutrients

major / open / confirmed; issue key: culturemech-intake-binding-0e46605eb9c0c439.

The complete descriptor provides only Micronutrients with a variable quantity. The selected CHEBI:27027 owner is a functional micronutrient role, not an identified constituent or defined preparation. The inspected primary ChEBI page distinguishes has-role relations from material identity. Preserve the source mention without inventing constituents or treating lexical synonymy as exact material equivalence.

Disposition: The complete unchanged case is quarantined; inspected member hashes and actual blocking witnesses remain in the durable decision JSON.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- A human-readable locator in case b198a7c5 contains a descriptor-hash transcription typo; the inspected_descriptor_sha256 list and complete case pin are correct. Independent cross-review is reconciling that locator separately without altering this frozen observation.
- Report-consolidation timestamps are recorded here; the case-level scientific inspection occurred during the preceding2026-11-16/17 session and is preserved in the explicit decisions and source locators.
- Canonical eligibility is the separately pinned native2026-09-21 mapping review; this review does not create a new global SSSOM release or adopt the active campaign.
- Identity support excludes dose, growth, whole-stock equivalence and unspecified supplied forms except where the individual reason explicitly establishes the scoped material correspondence.
- Per-case WITHHOLD inspected hashes are the authoritative minimum coverage; a blocking witness may quarantine an aggregate without claiming unread payloads were reviewed.
- Prepared intake, full owner packets and original producer remain immutable ignored evidence; the durable decisions retain their exact hashes and source pins. Final native intake finalization and independent product/QC verification remain required.

findings:
- finding_id: withhold-0e46605eb9c0c439
  issue_key: culturemech-intake-binding-0e46605eb9c0c439
  category: identity
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Source binding withheld: Micronutrients'
  description: The complete descriptor provides only Micronutrients with a variable
    quantity. The selected CHEBI:27027 owner is a functional micronutrient role, not
    an identified constituent or defined preparation. The inspected primary ChEBI
    page distinguishes has-role relations from material identity. Preserve the source
    mention without inventing constituents or treating lexical synonymy as exact material
    equivalence.
  target_ids:
  - case-0e46605eb9c0c439
  evidence_ids:
  - evidence-0e46605eb9c0c439
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    path: data/ingredients/mapped/Micronutrients.yaml
    role: Maintained candidate identity; frozen source corrections belong in CultureMech
  field_paths:
  - /preferred_term
  disposition_reason: The complete unchanged case is quarantined; inspected member
    hashes and actual blocking witnesses remain in the durable decision JSON.
  external_issues:
  - https://github.com/CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech/issues/1473
- finding_id: withhold-0e699d0582cad542
  issue_key: culturemech-intake-binding-0e699d0582cad542
  category: identity
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: 'Source binding withheld: Methylphosphonic acid'
  description: The complete Carini2014 assay event explicitly preserves the source
    substrate label while leaving salt/protonation equivalence unresolved. Its 1.0micromolar
    quantity and alternate-phosphorus experiment are supported, but the chosen CHEBI:45129
    acid identity is not independently certified by that event. Withhold source-form
    binding while preserving the event and separation from other phosphorus substrates.
  target_ids:
  - case-0e699d0582cad542
  evidence_ids:
  - evidence-0e699d0582cad542
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
